  - But the Black Rook is at c2. If King is at g1, the Rook can move to g2 and give check? Yes, Rc2-g2+.
  - Or even better, can the Rook check g1 from c1? Rc2-c1. No, that's a Rook move. If King is at g1, Rc1 is a check! (Rank 1).
  - So if f2g1, Black can play Rc2-c1+ or Rc2-g2+.

  - Black Rook can play Rc2-c1+ (Rank 1).

  - Black Rook is at c2. Does it attack g3? No.
  - Black Rook can move to c3 (Rank 3) and check the King at g3. Rc2-c3+.
  - Also, Black Queen at e4 can move to f3? (e->f is 1, 4->3 is 1). Yes! Diagonal.

  - However, the Queen at e4 can move to g4, which would check the King at g3? Yes, g4 is adjacent to g3.
  - Or the Queen could move to e3, which checks g3? No.

King at g3.
Black Rook c2: can move to c3+ (check).
Black Queen e4: can move to g4+ (check), or e3+ (check), or f3.

King at g1.

Black Queen e4: can move to f4 (not a check), e1+ (check).

- Rook can move to c3+
- Queen can move to g4+ or e3+

- Rook can move to c1+ or g2+
- Queen can move to e1+
If King is at f1:
- Rook can move to c1+ or f2+
- Queen can move to e1+
